# Lead register: all 45 university records (30 October 2026)

**Status of every record: unverified. 0 of 45 profiles are verified, 0 are partially verified.** Every entry below is a **lead**: a search-engine result (title, URL and the search tool's summary), not a page that was opened and read. Under `UNIVERSITY_EVIDENCE_STANDARD.md` (§1.3, §4, §6) a search snippet or summary never counts as evidence, however many agree; verification needs an exact quotation taken from the official source itself, and fees count only from the university's own pages. All 45 profiles therefore stay research previews: noindex, outside the sitemap, no verified fact shown. Nothing in this register was copied into a university record or an evidence file.

**Why only leads.** From this environment, official university websites, EDEBO (`registry.edbo.gov.ua`), the Ministry (`mon.gov.ua`), NAQA (`naqa.gov.ua`) and every other non-allowed host fail at DNS or are refused by the proxy (probes of 29 October: 0 of 88 official hosts, 0 of 14 secondary registries). The web search tool works, so official pages and register entries could be **found** but not **opened**. A refused connection is an access limitation, never evidence that an institution or a fact does not exist.

**Method.** The web search tool allows 200 searches per turn, shared by every agent. Round 1 (nine researchers) used the whole allowance on the first 18 records (8 to 15 searches each); its nine checkers could not search at all, so they compared every value with the extract it cites and recorded 97 corrections (a value its source does not show, a missed conflict, a classification stated too strongly), applied below. Round 2 researched the remaining records with at most 6 searches each, on the decisive fields first (register listing and Ukrainian name, official website and domain evidence, international admissions page, fees on the official domain, accreditation), and one checker re-searched the official website and register code of the first records (33 values re-found as recorded; 13 corrections or notes applied). Round 3 (6 independent checkers, 48 searches) checked every round-2 record twice: each value against the extracts it cites, then a re-search of the register code, Ukrainian name and official website (114 values re-found as recorded; 135 corrections applied). 367 research searches in all, plus the checkers' searches. Each field records the result URLs and what the results showed, compared with the existing record, with conflicts, open questions and the official URLs to capture.

**Contact details.** Phone numbers and the part of an e-mail address before the @ are not reproduced in this public register (they appear as "[phone number]" and "[e-mail on <domain>]"). A contact is published only after it is read on the captured official page. The domain is kept because it is evidence about which domain the institution uses.

**Classification used (owner's five categories):**

| Category | Profiles |
|---|---|
| 1. Verified by authoritative evidence | 0 |
| 2. Partially verified | 0 |
| 3. Secondary-source evidence only (leads found) | 14 |
| 4. Unverified (no useful leads) | 0 |
| 5. Conflicting or outdated evidence (leads disagree) | 31 |
| Not researched in this pass (search allowance used up) | 0 |

The category is derived from the fields, not from a researcher's own label: "conflicting" when any field still holds conflicting leads after the checks, "secondary-source evidence only" when any lead was found, "unverified" when a researched record found none. Category 3 and 5 records are not more verified than category 4; they only give more to check.

**How to turn a lead into evidence.** Route B (owner captures): save each URL in `research/verification/CAPTURE_LIST_2026-10-30.txt` as a PDF print or full-page screenshot with the address and date visible, keep the files outside the repository and share the folder; the entries are then written from the captures (`UNIVERSITY_EVIDENCE_STANDARD.md` §11.3). Route A: allow the hosts in the session's network settings: `ALLOWLIST_ALL.txt`, plus 35 hosts that appear only in these leads (`2med.karazin.ua`, `chempharm.onu.edu.ua`, `cie.karazin.ua`, `college.nuph.edu.ua`, `cpo.karazin.ua`, `diit.ust.edu.ua`, `dimh.edu.ua`, `foreign.nmuofficial.com`, `international.ieu.edu.ua`, `maem.edu.ua`, `mediacenter.uzhnu.edu.ua`, `medinstytut.lviv.ua`, `mu.od.ua`, `registry.naqa.gov.ua`, `start.karazin.ua`, `teach.cdu.edu.ua`, `tovaroved.nuph.edu.ua`, `umss.edu.ua`, `ust.edu.ua`, `vstup.dimh.edu.ua`, `vstup.dmu.edu.ua`, `vstup.knu.ua`, `vstup.maem.edu.ua`, `vstup.meduniv.lviv.ua`, `vstup.onu.edu.ua`, `vstup.umss.edu.ua`, `vstup.ust.edu.ua`, `vstup.vnu.edu.ua`, `www.kmu.gov.ua`, `www.kpi.kharkov.ua`, `www.kspu.edu`, `www.lsmu.edu.ua`, `www.med.karazin.ua`, `www.ombudsman.gov.ua`, `www.zhim.org.ua`). They are not added to the allowlist files because nothing yet shows that each belongs to the institution; opening them is how that is checked.

**Conflicts:**
- Location: the record says Dnipro. EDEBO 4250 gives a Vinnytsia legal address (вул. Янгеля Академіка, 4), and dimh.edu.ua says the institute relocated to Vinnytsia in May 2025.
- Website: the record uses dimh.kmu.edu.ua (a Kyiv Medical University subdomain). EDEBO names dimh.edu.ua.
- International Department e-mail: the record's FAQ reference gives [e-mail on kmu.edu.ua]. The dimh.edu.ua contact page gives [e-mail on dimh.edu.ua].
- Intake windows disagree: the old apply page gives 1 Jul–15 Dec and 15 Jan–15 Apr; the assessment report gives 14.07–01.11 and before 15.04; the FAQ (July 2026) defers to the International Department.
- 2025 NAQA accreditation: one search summary cites a 2025 NAQA record (en.naqa.gov.ua/?p=5914), while another says no confirmed 2025 decision was found. Certificates are issued for one year at a time.
- Fees: the payment page says 2025–2026 fees exist (amounts unseen). The only figures come from a 2025-02 assessment report, in USD per semester.

**Open questions:**
- Is Vinnytsia (вул. Академіка Янгеля, 4) the current teaching base for international students, and has the 'Dnipro' name or the legal form changed?
- Is dimh.kmu.edu.ua still an official institute domain, or legacy hosting by Kyiv Medical University?
- Is the Medicine (222/I2) AHPGS certificate in force for 2026/27 (2024 and 2025 NAQA decisions)? Are Dentistry and Pharmacy accredited or licensed?
- What do the EDEBO licence tables show (licensed volume for I1/I2/I8)? What is the MON licence decision?
- What is the 2026/27 tuition fee on https://dimh.edu.ua/en/payment/ (amount, currency, period)?
- What are the 2026/27 application deadlines for international applicants?
- What is Kyiv Medical University's legal relationship to the institute (co-founder?)?

- Entrance thresholds: the foreigner page says 140 points in biology, while the 2026 domestic rules use thresholds of 150 for Medicine and 140 for Pharmacy. These are probably different regimes.
- The preparatory department start date already conflicts in the record (1 September vs 1 October).

Володимира Вернадського, 9) corroborates that street. It is not a new address. The same applies to the officialContacts note's 'address ... new'.
- round-1 checker (no search possible: compared values with their cited extracts): value not in cited result — The value lists 'І7 Терапія та реабілітація (bachelor's)' among the 2026 admissions codes. No cited source shows it. The vstup.dmu.edu.ua sources given are i2-meditsina and i8-pharmacy only.
- round-1 checker (no search possible: compared values with their cited extracts): conflict missed — The value says no result confirms Dentistry for foreigners and that the Dean's Office names only Medicine. Results cited in other fields of the same record cut against this. The foreigner portal has a Dental studies master's page (study.dmu.edu.ua/dental-studies). The foreigner admission page sets 'not lower than 140 points in biology' for the 'general medicine' and 'dentistry' specialties (study.dmu.edu.ua/admission). The foreigner-portal accreditation page lists Dentistry. This is not proof of an English-medium Dentistry degree, but the tension belongs in the conflicts list.
- round-1 checker (no search possible: compared values with their cited extracts): value not in cited result — The agency figures (about USD 4,200–4,212 per year; USD 4,000–5,500; USD 3,500–5,000; hostel USD 800–1,500) are reported, but the extract itself says 'the summary does not tie each figure to a URL'. Only mbbsadmissionabroad.in is cited, and no figure can be attributed to it. Under the rule that every value needs its result URL and text, these figures must be removed or each tied to the result that showed it. They are correctly labelled third-party and not shown as official. Separately, the 80,072 UAH figure: the extract says only that it 'relate[s] to Dnipro State Medical University'. It does not name Medicine. The URL path (y2025) points to the 2025 campaign, not the current year.
- round-1 checker (no search possible: compared values with their cited extracts): other — newVsRecord says 'new', but the payment rule (first-year students pay tuition and hostel for the whole year in full, then by semester) is already in the record's accommodation note: 'Snippet (official payment terms, undated): first-year foreign students pay tuition and hostel fees for the whole year in full, and from the second year by semester.'
- round-1 checker (no search possible: compared values with their cited extracts): value not in cited result — The value calls the dates 'entrance exam windows'. The cited study.dmu.edu.ua/admission-s/admission-process extract says only 'one admissions page gives August 26 to October 30 inclusive and February 2–27 for spring entry'. It does not say what the windows are for.

**Limitations:** No official, EDEBO, MON or NAQA page was opened; every value comes from search-engine summaries and titles. Many foreigner-portal pages are reported as 1,000–2,000 days old. Fees appear only on third-party agency sites, and the summary does not tie each figure to a URL. A search that found nothing does not show that something does not exist.

- Leadership: EDEBO names acting rector Майя Єрмолаєва; a Suspilne item (probably 2025) quotes university head Олександр Герасименко; Wikipedia and the record mention Petro Kondratenko.
- Language: an older International Medical Faculty page says English and Russian; the 2026/27 timetable says English; the record lists English and Ukrainian.
- Licensed volume: 856 (2017 aggregator record) vs 1,044 (2025 aggregator record).
- Foreign student numbers: 721 from 43 countries (old official page) vs 400 foreign out of 800 at the Kropyvnytskyi branch (Suspilne, undated) vs other figures in the record.

- Are first-year foreign applicants admitted for 2026/27, to which programmes, in English, and with what deadlines?
- What licensed volumes appear in the EDEBO licence table at /university/4/specialities?

The summary for the successor entry registry.edbo.gov.ua/university/7411 gives 'сайт — feu.edu.ua'. After the merger, ieu.edu.ua may be the former entity's site. This is an unresolved conflict and must not be shown as current.

**Limitations:** No official page, register page or NAQA page was opened (hosts unreachable). Every value comes from search-engine summaries of results. One summary mixed the 4374 and 7411 founding dates, and another suggested feu.edu.ua without that host appearing as a result. Fee and admission pages on ieu.edu.ua carry no academic year and may be outdated by the 20.07.2026 EDEBO block. The Malta closure and Poland claims rest on a single third-party article plus the MFHEA notice already in the record.

**Conflicts:**
- Founding year: the EDEBO lead gives 03.10.2017; agent pages give 2017, 2018 or even 1934 (third-party summary of agent sites).
- Deadlines: the official requirements page gives arrival windows of 1 June–30 October (summer intake) and 10 January–28 February (winter intake); the Study in Ukraine portal shows 'until 1 February' and 'until 1 September'. Neither states the year.
- Places: official 2026 admission volume for Medicine is 130 (khimu.edu.ua/vstup/obsiah-pryyomu/); vstup.osvita.ua shows licensed volume 118 for one 2026 Medicine offer. These may be different measures; unresolved.
- Award naming: the official English homepage summary calls the programme 'General Medicine (MBBS)'. The record's rule is never 'MBBS' unless the university uses it, so the exact wording must be read on the page; the award title in the programme document may differ.
- Fees: official-domain lead of about 4,000 USD per year (no academic year) versus 42,750 UAH per year on vstup.osvita.ua (third party, older, probably the domestic contract). They are not comparable and neither is verified.
- Accreditation terminology: uniRank's 'accreditation by Ministry of Health since 2022' conflicts with how Ukraine separates licensing (Ministry or EDEBO) from accreditation (NAQA).

**Open questions:**
- Is the Medicine (I2/222) educational programme accredited by NAQA? Only a 2022 Dentistry certificate (No. 3483, third-party lead) and an EQAR institutional record that expired in 2021 were found.
- Licence record and licensed volumes for Medicine and Dentistry on the EDEBO card (registry.edbo.gov.ua/university/4054): the page was not opened.
- Current (2026/27) teaching format and teaching site in Kharkiv: no official statement found.
- Does Dentistry admit foreign applicants, and in which language?
- Academic year, payment period and schedule for the official ~4,000 USD fee lead; does the 2026/27 fee page exist?
- Exact 2026 deadlines for foreign applicants; how the official arrival windows reconcile with the Study in Ukraine portal dates.
- Contents of the 2026 Regulation on admission of foreigners listed on the normative-documents page.
- Any Pharmacy (226) or Nursing programme: none found.
- Exact English award title (MBBS wording versus the programme document).

- Record-internal: Dentistry 221 level lead is master, while a cited third-party aggregator (findcourse.com) labels it Bachelor Dentistry. The aggregator is a lead only and is not an official source.

An open question also asks to 'confirm the government register entry (university/85) names this institution and that website'. Yet the same record's other reference for https://universities.studyinukraine.gov.ua/en/minedu/university/85/contacts says it was 'summarised as a different university, not OIMU'. And research/verification/CAPTURE_REQUEST_2026-10-12.md row 24b files university/85/contacts under odesa-national-medical-university (the state ONMedU). The 'Register entry' label is also too strong. A Study in Ukraine portal page is a directory listing, not an EDEBO, licence or NAQA register entry; the NUPh record treats its portal ID 41 that way. The domainOwnership note mentions only the 'different university' summary. Neither note flags the conflict.
- round-3 checker (compared with the cited extracts, then re-searched): url not found in any result — https://universities.studyinukraine.gov.ua/en/minedu/university/85/contacts is not in any cited source for this institution. Worse, my Study in Ukraine searches (2026-10-30, allowed_domains universities.studyinukraine.gov.ua) returned https://universities.studyinukraine.gov.ua/en/minedu/university/85/ under the title 'Odesa National Medical University', and the summary gave onmedu.edu.ua as its website. ID 85 is therefore a lead for a different institution (ONMedU, EDEBO 188). Capturing it as OIMU evidence would attach ONMedU's listing to OIMU. Remove it. No Study in Ukraine listing for OIMU was returned, which is not evidence that none exists. (removed from the capture list: https://universities.studyinukraine.gov.ua/en/minedu/university/85/contacts)
- round-3 checker (compared with the cited extracts, then re-searched): value not in cited result — The value names IAAR programmes '4074 and 4075'. The only IAAR source cited is https://iaar.agency/registry/program/4075/en (title 'International Medical University (Not Accredited)'); 4074 appears in no cited result. The attribution 'probably concern a different institution' is an inference: the extract is a title only.
- round-3 checker (compared with the cited extracts, then re-searched): other — The value counts 'the record's MON annex of October 2021' among the latest dated leads. The record's research reference for that annex (mon.gov.ua/.../2021/october/227-dodatki.pdf) says it was 'returned for the Ukrainian name; relevance to this institution not confirmed'. Using it to push the activity timeline to October 2021 overstates the record. The oimu.com.ua page is also on a domain that has not been confirmed as official.

**Limitations:** No official, EDEBO, MON or NAQA page was opened (the network blocks them). The oimu.edu.ua allowed-domains search returned no results, and Ukrainian- and English-language status searches surfaced only other institutions, an encyclopaedia and its mirrors. Fees, deadlines, documents and entry requirements were not searched specifically, because no indexed official domain was found. Search #5 returned only Odesa National Medical University material, which is recorded under that institution. All searches used standard mode.

The recorded value gives the portal address as 'Gagarina Avenue, Dnipro, 49000' with no number. EDEBO gives просп. Науки, 72, so both sources use house number 72. Keep 'conflicting leads': nothing shows a renaming. Add the house number to the portal half of the value.

**Limitations:** Six WebSearch calls (standard mode). No official page, register card or PDF was opened; every value is a search-engine summary of a result, so it is a lead and never evidence. The summaries may paraphrase, misattribute or show stale register data (the summary itself warns the rector entry may be outdated). The NAQA schedule row could not be tied to a specific returned document except by matching column headers. A failed or empty search is not evidence that something does not exist.

- Founding: the EDEBO summary gives 1944 as the founding year, while the institute's English 'About us' page is summarised as describing a 2020 municipal institution. This may be a reorganisation date and is unresolved.
- English name wording: the EDEBO lead 'Municipal Institution Of Higher Education "Volyn Medical Institute" Of The Volyn Oblast Council' differs from the record's own unofficial translation ('Municipal Higher Education Institution ... of the Volyn Regional Council').
- Fee year attribution: the official-domain 2025-2026 fee PDF shows a range up to 45,850 UAH, while the aggregator figures in the record attribute 45,850 UAH (master) and 36,600 UAH (bachelor) to 2026.
- Ownership: the EDEBO governing body (oblast council) agrees with the communal legal name, against osvita.ua's 'Державна' (record red flag). The register's ownership-form field was not quoted.

- Do the 2026 admission rules (pravila-priyomu) contain any section on foreign citizens? Is there any international intake or English-medium teaching at all?

- Is the acting-rector status current?
- What are the official contacts and the international-office details?
- Relevance: the institute is nursing-level only, with no medicine, dentistry or pharmacy degree lead. The name-confusion risk with Lesya Ukrainka Volyn National University stands.

**Conflicts:**
- Public Health: the official-domain fee page lists a master's in Громадське здоров'я (full-time and part-time), while the record holds Public Health only as an FRSE-directory claim at junior-bachelor level.
- Founding: the EDEBO register founding date is 02.08.1954, while the record's 2017 MON news says the institute was created in 2017 on the basis of the Zhytomyr Institute of Nursing, and the FRSE directory claims an 1875 origin. The dates appear to mean different things; unresolved.
- Contacts: the EDEBO email is a free-mail address carrying the predecessor 'nursing' name ([e-mail on gmail.com]), while the register and the Ministry portal give zhim.org.ua as the website.
- Foreign-student page: the official-domain page with a foreign-applicant document list carries a 2012–2017 copyright, and no current statement was found. It may be stale.
- Fee table year: the page title says 2025/2026 but also lists 2026/27–2028/29 amounts, which are cohort instalments. A college document dates its tables to Academic Council protocol No 10 of 24.04.2025. Neither is the 2026 entrant fee.

**Open questions:**
- Does the institute admit foreign citizens now? Read the current Rules of Admission, the regulation on foreign citizens (polozhenja.php) and international_stud.php, and get a dated statement.
- What does the EDEBO licence table for code 319 show: specialties, levels and licensed volumes (337 vs 394 in catalogues)? Is Public Health (master's) licensed, and is Технології медичної діагностики та лікування licensed?
- Is there NAQA accreditation for the 223 Nursing bachelor and master programmes and for the other programmes? What did the self-assessment for the Nursing master's lead to?
- What are the current fees for 2026 entrants, from the 'Абітурієнту 2026' page? Is there any fee for foreign applicants?
- What are the 2026 admission stages and dates (etapy_vstupnoi_kampanii.php)?
- Does Dentistry exist at higher-education level, or only at the college sub-unit (EDEBO 1884)? Is Management still offered?
- What are the official contacts on zhim.org.ua, given that the register email is free-mail?
- Is the acting-rector status current?
- Relevance: there is no medicine, dentistry or pharmacy degree lead. The name-confusion risk with Zhytomyr Polytechnic, Polissia National University, the Zhytomyr Institute of MAUP and the predecessor Zhytomyr Institute of Nursing stands.
